# Artists in the MoMA collection

Every constituent in MoMA's public `Artists.csv` (snapshot of 2026-08-29), 15,934 entries, sorted by name. MoMA's list counts everyone credited on a work, so it includes architects, designers, photographers and companies as well as artists. Works and paintings are counted from `Artworks.csv`; a blank date means MoMA has none.

- 14,699 have at least one work in `Artworks.csv`
- 1,106 have at least one painting

Generated by `python3 fetch_data.py --artists` — do not edit by hand.
